Based on our previous attempts, we know that some search-related buttons didn't work as expected when the search bar was empty. Now that we have a query, the behavior might change. The most logical action is to press the "Search" button on the keyboard, which acts like an "Enter" key.

Looking at the UI information, there are two widgets with the feature 'Search'. The one on the keyboard (element #50, `id=key_pos_ime_action`) is the one we want to press to initiate the search.
